Theorem 213.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: The magnet variety V defined by convergence analysis constraints admits a stratification into 662 smooth components. Each component has Euler characteristic χ ≤ 888 and intersection form of rank 2.

Proof: We construct the required map explicitly. Starting from the convergence analysis condition on magnet structure S, we define the operator T by T(x) = Σ c_k x^k where |c_k| ≤ 662. The convergence follows from the convergence analysis estimate ||T^n|| ≤ 888. QED.
